The vulnerabilities are listed below -   CVE-2025-43529  (CVSS score: N/A) - A use-after-free vulnerability in WebKit that may lead to arbitrary code execution when processing maliciously crafted web content  CVE-2025-14174  (CVSS score: 8.8) - A memory corruption issue in WebKit that may lead to memory corruption when processing maliciously crafted web content   Apple said it's aware that the shortcomings "may have been exploited in an extremely sophisticated attack against specific targeted individuals on versions of iOS before iOS 26." <input type='checkbox' name='article_1362' value='https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/fake-osint-and-gpt-utility-github-repos.html' /> 1362 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+Fake+OSINT+and+GPT+Utility+GitHub+Repos+Spread+PyStoreRAT+Malware+Payloads%EE%A0%82Dec+12%2C+2025Malware+%2F+CryptocurrencyCybersecurity+researchers+are+calling+attention+to+a+new+campaign+that%27s+leveraging+GitHub-hosted+Python+repositories+to+distribute+a+previously+undocumented+JavaScript-based+Remote+Access+Trojan+%28RAT%29+dubbed+PyStoreRAT+.++%22These+repositories%2C+often+themed+as+development+utilities+or+OSINT+tools%2C+contain+only+a+few+lines+of+code+responsible+for+silently+downloading+a+remote+HTA+file+and+executing+it+via+%27mshta.exe%2C%27%22+Morphisec+researcher+Yonatan+Edri+said++in+a+report+shared+with+The+Hacker+News.++PyStoreRAT+has+been+described+as+a+%22modular%2C+multi-stage%22+implant+that+can+execute+EXE%2C+DLL%2C+PowerShell%2C+MSI%2C+Python%2C+JavaScript%2C+and+HTA+modules.+The+malware+also+deploys+an+information+stealer+known+as+Rhadamanthys+as+a+follow-on+payload.++Attack+chains+involve+distributing+the+malware+through+Python+or+JavaScript+loader+stubs+embedded+in+GitHub+repositories+masquerading+as+OSINT+tools%2C+DeFi+bots%2C+GPT+wrappers%2C+and+security-themed+utilities+that+are+designed...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/fake-osint-and-gpt-utility-github-repos.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fake OSINT and GPT Utility GitHub Repos Spread PyStoreRAT Malware PayloadsDec 12, 2025Malware / CryptocurrencyCybersecurity researchers are calling attention to a new campaign that's leveraging GitHub-hosted Python repositories to distribute a previously undocumented JavaScript-based Remote Access Trojan (RAT) dubbed PyStoreRAT .  "These repositories, often themed as development utilities or OSINT tools, contain only a few lines of code responsible for silently downloading a remote HTA file and executing it via 'mshta.exe,'" Morphisec researcher Yonatan Edri said  in a report shared with The Hacker News.  PyStoreRAT has been described as a "modular, multi-stage" implant that can execute EXE, DLL, PowerShell, MSI, Python, JavaScript, and HTA modules. The malware also deploys an information stealer known as Rhadamanthys as a follow-on payload.  Attack chains involve distributing the malware through Python or JavaScript loader stubs embedded in GitHub repositories masquerading as OSINT tools, DeFi bots, GPT wrappers, and security-themed utilities that are designed...</a><br>

<input type='checkbox' name='article_1363' value='https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/new-advanced-phishing-kits-use-ai-and.html' /> 1363 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+New+Advanced+Phishing+Kits+Use+AI+and+MFA+Bypass+Tactics+to+Steal+Credentials+at+Scale%EE%A0%82Dec+12%2C+2025Malware+%2F+Email+SecurityCybersecurity+researchers+have+documented+four+new+phishing+kits+named+BlackForce%2C+GhostFrame%2C+InboxPrime+AI%2C+and+Spiderman++that+are+capable+of+facilitating+credential+theft+at+scale.++BlackForce%2C+first+detected+in+August+2025%2C+is+designed+to+steal+credentials+and+perform+Man-in-the-Browser+%28+MitB+%29+attacks+to+capture+one-time+passwords+%28OTPs%29+and+bypass+multi-factor+authentication+%28MFA%29.+The+kit+is+sold+on+Telegram+forums+for+anywhere+between+%E2%82%AC200+%28%24234%29+and+%E2%82%AC300+%28%24351%29.++The+kit%2C+according+to+Zscaler+ThreatLabz++researchers+Gladis+Brinda+R+and+Ashwathi+Sasi%2C+has+been+used+to+impersonate+over+11+brands%2C+including+Disney%2C+Netflix%2C+DHL%2C+and+UPS.+It%27s+said+to+be+in+active+development.++%22BlackForce+features+several+evasion+techniques+with+a+blocklist+that+filters+out+security+vendors%2C+web+crawlers%2C+and+scanners%2C%22+the+company+said.+%22BlackForce+remains+under+active+development.+Version+3+was+widely+used+until+early+August%2C+with+versions+4+and+5+being+released+in+subsequ...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2025/12/new-advanced-phishing-kits-use-ai-and.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Advanced Phishing Kits Use AI and MFA Bypass Tactics to Steal Credentials at ScaleDec 12, 2025Malware / Email SecurityCybersecurity researchers have documented four new phishing kits named BlackForce, GhostFrame, InboxPrime AI, and Spiderman  that are capable of facilitating credential theft at scale.  BlackForce, first detected in August 2025, is designed to steal credentials and perform Man-in-the-Browser ( MitB ) attacks to capture one-time passwords (OTPs) and bypass multi-factor authentication (MFA). The kit is sold on Telegram forums for anywhere between €200 ($234) and €300 ($351).  The kit, according to Zscaler ThreatLabz  researchers Gladis Brinda R and Ashwathi Sasi, has been used to impersonate over 11 brands, including Disney, Netflix, DHL, and UPS. It's said to be in active development.  "BlackForce features several evasion techniques with a blocklist that filters out security vendors, web crawlers, and scanners," the company said. "BlackForce remains under active development. Version 3 was widely used until early August, with versions 4 and 5 being released in subsequ...</a><br>
